{D108}]@#/_/'phags pa lang kar gshegs pa rin po che'i mdo las sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsung gi snying po zhes bya ba'i le'u/_bam po dang po/_sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus 
[\[192a.2\]]gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das lho phyogs kyi rgya mtsho'i 'gram lang ka'i ri'i rtse mo rin po che'i me tog sna tshogs kyis bkod pa na/_dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i tshogs sangs rgyas kyi zhing sna tshogs tha dad pa 
[\[192a.3\]]nas 'dus pa dag dang thabs cig tu bzhugs te/_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag thams cad kyang tshad med pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dbang po dang /_stobs dang /_mngon par shes pas rnam par rtsen pa sha stag la/_byang chub sems 
[\[192a.4\]]dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po ni gtso bor gyur te/_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi phyag gis spyi bo nas dbang bskur bas dbang bskur ba/_rang gi sems snang ba'i spyod yul gyi don la rab tu mkhas pa/_sems can sna tshogs kyi sems dang /_gzugs 
[\[192a.5\]]sna tshogs dang /_'dul ba'i sgo tshad med pa rnams ji lta ba bzhin du kun du ston pa/_chos lnga dang /_rang bzhin dang /_rnam par shes pa dang /_bdag med pa gnyis rtogs par khong du chud pa'o/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' blo gros chen po/_byang

[\[192a.7\]]nas bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod do/_/'jig rten skye 'jig yongs bral te/_/nam mkha'i me tog rab tu 'dra/_/ye shes yod med mi dmigs kyang /_/thugs rje chen po rab tu skye/_/chos rnams thams cad sgyu ma

[\[192b.1\]]bzhin/_/sems dang rnam shes yongs su spangs/_/ye shes yod med mi dmigs kyang /_/thugs rje chen po rab tu skye/_/rtag dang chad pa yongs spangs pa/_/'jig rten khor zug rmi lam 'dra/_/ye shes yod med mi dmigs kyang /_/thugs rje chen po rab tu skye/_/
[\[192b.2\]]gang zag chos la bdag med dang /_/nyon mongs shes bya rab mkhyen cing /_/rtag tu dag cing mtshan med kyang /_/thugs rje chen po rab tu skye/_/kun las mya ngan mi 'da' ste/_/mya ngan 'das sangs rgyas mi dmigs/_/sangs rgyas mya ngan 'da' mi dmigs/_/blo dang 
[\[192b.3\]]rig par bgyi dang bral/_/yod pa dang ni med pa'i phyogs/_/gnyis po de dag shin tu spangs/_/thub pa zhi ba'i rnal 'byor pa/_/skye ba dag ni rab spangs pas/_/de ni len mi mnga' zhes bgyi/_/'di dang gzhan du rnam par dag_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' blo gros chen

[\[192b.5\]]rten mkhyen pa'i skyes mchog gis/_/tshigs bcad de ni gsan pa dang /_/'khor rnams la ni rnam gzigs nas/_/rgyal sras rnams la bka' stsal pa/_/khyod la sogs pa rgyal sras rnams/_/ci dang ci 'dod dris shig dang /_/so so rang gi spyod yul ni/_/

[\[192b.7\]]dran pa 'phel bar 'gyur/_/lta rmongs nor pa ji lta bu/_/nor pa ji ltar 'phel bar 'gyur/_/zhing du sprul rnams ci slad du/_/mtshan nyid dang ni mu stegs kyang /_/mtshan med rim pa ji lta bu/_/ci slad mtshan ma med pas bgyi/_/ci slad rgyal ba'i sras zhes 
[\[193a\]]
[\[193a.1\]]bgyi/_/rnam grol gang du mchi bar 'gyur/_/gang la 'ching zhing gang dag dgrol/_/bsam gtan spyod yul ji lta bu/_/ji slad theg pa gsum po mchis/_/bdag la bstan cing bshad du gsol/_/rkyen rnams gang las skye ba lags/_/byed 
[\[193a.2\]]dang byas pa ji lta bu/_/gnyi ga tha dad ci slad bshad/_/ji lta bur na 'phel ba lags/_/gzugs med snyoms par 'jug pa dang /_/'gog pa'i snyoms 'jug ji lta bu/_/'du shes 'gog pa ji lta bu/_/ting nge 'dzin las ji ltar sad/_/ci yi slad du byas pa 
[\[193a.3\]]'byung /_/'gro ba dang ni lus 'dzin ci/_/ji ltar rnam rtog snang bar 'gyur/_/sa rnams ji ltar skye bar 'gyur/_/srid gsum 'jig pa de su zhig_/gnas ni gang lags lus ni ci/_/skye gnas gang du bgrod par 'gyur/_/rgyal ba'i sras su ji ltar 'gyur/_/

[\[193a.5\]]mthong nas slar yang ldog_/ji lta bur na rigs su 'gyur/_/rigs ma mchis dang sems tsam dang /_/sgro 'dogs mtshan nyid ji lta bu/_/bdag med don du ji ltar 'gyur/_/sems can ma mchis ji lta bu/_/kun rdzob bshad pa ji lta bu/_/ji ltar rtag dang chad 
[\[193a.6\]]pa yi/_/lta ba rab tu 'byung mi 'gyur/_/ji ltar sangs rgyas mu stegs dag_/mtshan nyid rnams kyis 'gal mi 'gyur/_/ji ltar ma 'ongs dus dag na/_/sde pa tha dad 'byung bar 'gyur/_/ji ltar stong nyid rgyu ci yis/_/ji ltar skad cig 'jig par 'gyur/_/

[\[193b\]]
[\[193b.1\]]rnams kyang /_/yul rnams ci slad 'khrug par 'gyur/_/ci slad srid par lta bar bgyid/_/ji ltar skye 'gag ma mchis shing /_/'jig rten nam mkha' me tog 'dra/_/'jig rten ji ltar thugs su chud/_/ci slad yi ge bral bar gsung /_/rnam rtog spangs pa de su 
[\[193b.2\]]lags/_/ji ltar nam mkha' 'dra ba lags/_/de kho na la du zhig mchis/_/pha rol phyin sems rnam pa du/_/rgyu gang gis ni sa rnams sgrol/_/len ma mchis par su zhig mchi/_/bdag med rnam gnyis gang zhig lags/_/shes bya ji ltar rnam par 'dag_/ye 
[\[193b.3\]]shes dag ni rnam pa du/_/tshul khrims sems can nyid ni du/_/nor bu mu tig la sogs pa'i/_/rigs rnams su yis rab tu bskyed/_/sems can rnam pa sna tshogs dang /_/brjod pa dag ni su yis bskyed/_/rig pa'i gnas dang bzo yi rnams/_/su yis 
[\[193b.4\]]ji ltar rab tu bstan/_/tshigs su bcad pa rnam pa du/_/tshigs bcad ring thung ji lta bu/_/rigs pa rnam pa du zhig mchis/_/ci yi slad du smra zhes bgyi/_/skom dang zas rnams ji ltar 'byung /_/ji ltar sred pa'i 'dod pa 'byung /_/ci slad rgyal po zhes bgyi lags/_/

[\[193b.6\]]dpon du yang ji ltar 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas rnam pa du zhig lags/_/skyes pa'i rabs la rnam pa du/_/bdud dang slob pa'i bye brag rnams/_/de dag la ni rnam pa du/_/rang bzhin dang ni sems rnams kyang /_/de dag la ni du zhig mchis/_/btags pa tsam

[\[194b.1\]]ni gang /_/rgyal ba'i sras ni de dag dang /_/rang rgyal dang ni nyan thos dag_/rgyu gang gis na 'gyur brgyar 'gyur/_/rgyu gang gis ni snang med brgya/_/'jig rten mngon shes ji lta bu/_/'jig rten 'das kyi'ang ji lta bu/_/sems ni sa rnams bdun por yang /_/ci ltar ci slad bdag la

[\[195a.1\]]bsam gtan rnams dang tshad med dang /_/phung po lus rnams 'gro 'ong dang /_/snyoms par 'jug dang 'gog pa dang /_/ting 'dzin ldang sems bshad pa dang /_/sems dang yid dang rnam shes dang /_/bdag med pa dang chos lnga dang /_/rang bzhin rtog dang

[\[195b.1\]]kyi/_/sku la rdul phran du yod ces/_/de skad ci'i phyir 'dri mi byed/_/me lce la ni rdul phran du/_/rlung la yang ni rdul phran du/_/dbang po gcig cing rdul phran du/_/smin ma spu yi bu ga du/_/nor srung dbang phyug rgyal po dang /_/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po yang /_/rgyal srid dag

[\[195b.5\]]'du shes gcad par bya/_/ji ltar ting nge 'dzin las ldang /_/srid gsum bcom pa de su zhig_/gnas gang de yi lus kyang ci/_/ci phyir sems can med bzhin du/_/bdag yod par ni rjod par byed/_/kun rdzob bstan pa ji lta bu/_/rgya cher rab tu dbyer gsol zhes/_/

[\[196a.1\]]zhing rnams sna tshogs ci yi phyir/_/drang srong yun ring dka' thub dang /_/rigs zhes bya ba ji lta bu/_/bla ma gang las bslab pa blang /_/ci yi phyir na mi dag ngan/_/ci yi phyir na rnal 'byor pa/_/'dod pa'i khams su 'tshang mi rgya/_/'og min du ni grub

[\[196a.3\]]dri ba'i rigs/_/dgu po rgyud [{]mangs [,]mang [}]me tog rnga/_/zhing dang snang ba spangs pa dang /_/sems tsam sa ni bdun po dag_/dris pa rnams ni yang dag nyid/_/'di dang gzhan yang rab mang ba'i/_/dri ba dag ni rgyal sras dris/_/re re mtshan nyid rab ldan zhing /_/lta ba'i nyes pa rnam spangs pa/_/grub

[\[198a.7\]]long rab tu gsal bas gzugs brnyan rnams 'dzin par byed pa de bzhin du snang ba'i rnam par shes pa snang ba yang de bzhin no/_/blo gros chen po snang ba'i rnam par shes pa dang /_dngos po so sor rnam par rtog pa'i rnam par shes pa de gnyis ni 'jig pa dang /_

[\[198b.4\]]dang /_gang la dmigs par bya ba 'gags na des rgyun 'gag par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po de ci'i phyir zhe na/_de'i gnas yin pa'i phyir ro/_/de la gnas zhes bya ba ni thog ma med pa'i rnam par rtog pa srid pa gsum gyi sdug bsngal 'gags pa/_mi 
[\[198b.5\]]shes pa dang /_sred pa dang /_las dang /_rkyen rnam par log pa/_rang gi sems snang ba sgyu ma'i yul dang /_rjes su lta ba rnams bshad par bya'o/_/blo gros chen po dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze la la ni sa bon med pa'am/_sa bon yod pa'i rgyu las 'bras bu snang ba dang /_
[\[198b.6\]]dngos po'i dus dang /_gnas pa dang /_rkyen rnams las phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched rnams skye ba dang /_gnas pa'am/_yang na skyes nas kyang 'jig par 'gyur bar 'dod de/_blo gros chen po de dag ni rgyun nam/_bya ba'am/_skye ba'am/_srid 
[\[198b.7\]]pa'am/_mya ngan las 'das pa'am/_lam mam/_las sam/_'bras bu'am/_bden pa rnam par 'jig pa'am/_chad par smra bar 'gyur ro/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_gang 'di mngon sum du mi dmigs shing thog ma mi mthong ste/_de'i cha ma yin pa'i phyir ro/_/blo 
[\[199a\]]
[\[199a.1\]]gros chen po dper na rdza ma chag nas gyo mor gyur pa ni rdza ma'i bya ba mi byed do/_/sa bon tshig pa yang myu gu'i bya ba mi byed de/_de bzhin du blo gros chen po phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched kyi dngos po dag 'gags pa dang /_'gag pa dang /_

[\[199a.3\]]spu skye ba dang /_bye ma las kyang 'bru mar 'byung bar 'gyur ba'i rigs te/_'o na ni khyod kyi dam bcas pa las nyams shing nges pa'i don dang yang 'gal bar 'gyur ro/_/sa bon yod pa dang /_sa bon med par bshad pa la nyes pa de lta bu yod pas bya ba dang las byas 
[\[199a.4\]]pa rnams don med par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po mu stegs can de dag rkyen gsum phrad pa las skye zhes bya ba yang bya ba dang /_snang ba 'dus pa las skye ba ma yin gyi/_gud na tha dad pa'i rgyu yod do zhes smra'o/_/blo gros chen po rgyu de ni gtso bo dang /_mchog dang /_skyes 
[\[199a.5\]]bu dang /_dbang phyug dang /_dus dang /_rdul phra mo rnams so/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po rang bzhin rnam pa bdun yod de/_'di lta ste/_'du ba dngos po'i rang bzhin dang /_mtshan nyid kyi rang bzhin dang /_'byung ba chen po'i dngos po'i rang bzhin dang /_rgyu'i rang bzhin dang /_

[\[199a.7\]]'das pa'i spyod yul dang /_sras kyi sa las 'das pa'i spyod yul dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa nyid byon pa'i spyod yul te/_blo gros chen po 'di dag 'das pa dang /_ma 'ongs pa dang /_da ltar gyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 
[\[199b\]]
[\[199b.1\]]kyi dngos po'i rang bzhin don dam pa'i snying po'o/_/dngos po'i rang bzhin don dam pa'i snying po de dang ldan pas de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten pa dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa mchog gi chos 'phags pa'i shes rab kyi spyan yongs su 
[\[199b.2\]]rdzogs nas rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid rnam par dgod pa la 'jug cing ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dgod pas mu stegs can gyi smra ba dang /_lta ba ngan pa dang thun mong du mi 'gyur ba yin no/_/blo gros chen po ji ltar na mu stegs can gyi smra ba dang /_lta ba ngan pa dang /_thun mong 
[\[199b.3\]]ma yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rang gi yul la rnam par rtog pa'i lta bar khong du ma chud pa dang /_rang gi rnam par shes pa'i sems snang ba tsam du ma rtogs pas/_blo gros chen po byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams dngos po dang /_dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin don dam pa la lta 
[\[199b.4\]]ba gnyis su smra bar gyur to/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po dus kyi rnam par rtog pas yongs su bsgos pa'o/_/dmigs pa zhes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/_rang gi sems snang ba la rnam par shes pa'i yul du rnam par rtog pa'o/_/blo gros chen po dper na 'jim pa'i gong bu dang /_
[\[199b.5\]]rdul phra rab ni tha dad pa yang ma yin/_tha mi dad pa yang ma yin no/_/gser dang rgyan yang de bzhin te/_/blo gros chen po gal te 'jim pa'i gong bu dang /_rdul phra rab gnyis tha dad pa zhig yin na ni de dag las brtsams pa ma yin par 'gyur na/_de ni yang dag par 
[\[199b.6\]]de dag las brtsams pas na de'i phyir tha dad pa ma yin no/_/ci ste tha mi dad na ni 'jim pa'i gong bu dang /_rdul phra rab bye brag med par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du blo gros chen po 'jug pa'i rnam par shes pa rnams kun gzhi rnam par shes pa yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid 
[\[199b.7\]]dang /_gal te tha dad pa zhig yin na kun gzhi rnam par shes pa rgyu ma yin par 'gyur ro/_/de ste tha dad pa ma yin na ni 'jug pa'i rnam par shes pa 'gags na/_kun gzhi rnam par shes pa yang 'gag par 'gyur ba zhig na/_'di ltar rang gi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid 
[\[200a\]]
[\[200a.1\]]ni nges par 'gag par mi 'gyur te/_de'i blo gros chen po rnam par shes pa rnams kyi rang gi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid 'gag pa ma yin gyi/_las kyi mtshan nyid 'gag go/_/gal te rang gi yang dag pa'i mtshan nyid kyang 'gag pa yin na ni/_kun

[\[200a.3\]]yul rnams la 'dzin pa 'gags par gyur na/_rnam par shes pa'i rgyun 'gag par 'gyur ro zhes zer te/_gal te rnam par shes pa'i rgyun 'gags pa yin na ni/_thog ma med pa'i dus kyi rgyun kyang chad par 'gyur ba'i rigs so/_/blo gros chen po mu stegs can rnams 
[\[200a.4\]]rgyun skye ba'i rgyu ni mig gi rnam par shes pa gzugs dang /_thabs ni rgyu dang 'bras bu'i rang gi mtshan nyid du yod la 'das pa dang /_ma 'ongs pa dang /_da ltar byung ba ni sa bon yod pa dang /_sa bon med pa'i mtshan nyid do zhes zer te/_de ni sngon gcig nas gcig tu rigs pa'i 
[\[200a.5\]]dngos po brgyud de 'ongs pa rtog ge pa'i rjes su 'jug cing rang gi lta ba'i nyes pa'i bag chags kyis bsgos pas de skad du ston te/_blo gros chen po de ltar byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams lta ba ngan pas zin pa log pa'i gya gyus rab tu rmongs pa dang myos pa'i mi shes pa rnams [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]du 'di 
[\[200a.6\]]ni thams cad mkhyen pas bstan to zhes zer ro/_/blo gros chen po gal te yang dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze gang la la chos thams cad rang bzhin dang bral ba sprin dang mgal me'i 'khor lo dang /_dri za'i grong khyer dang mtshungs pa dang /_ma skyes pa sgyu ma dang /_smig rgyu dang /_chu'i 
[\[200a.7\]]zla ba dang /_rmi lam lta bu dang /_nang dang phyi rol du sems snang ba rnam par rtog pa dang /_thog ma med pa'i dus kyi rnam par spros pa rang gi sems kyi rnam par rtog pa las logs shig na med pa dang /_rgyu dang rkyen 'gags shing zad par gyur na/_rnam par rtog pa dang bral bar 
[\[200b\]]
[\[200b.1\]]mthong ba dang /_rjod pa dang /_brjod par bya ba dang /_brtag pa dang /_brtag par bya ba dang bral ba/_longs spyod dang /_lus su kun gzhi rnam par shes pa rnam par gzhag pa dang /_rnam par shes pa'i yul gzung ba dang /_'dzin pa dang /_mi 'thun pa dang /_snang ba med pa'i 
[\[200b.2\]]spyod yul skye ba dang /_gnas pa dang /_'jig pa spong ba dang /_rang gi sems las byung ba tsam du rjes su 'jug cing rnam par 'byed na/_blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa' de dag ni ring por mi thogs par 'khor ba dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa mnyam pa nyid dang /_
[\[200b.3\]]snying rje chen po dang /_thabs la mkhas pa dang /_rtsol ba med pa'i sbyor ba 'thob par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po sems can gyi khams de dag thams cad sgyu ma dang mtshungs pa dang /_rgyu dang rkyen la mi brtson pa dang /_phyi nang gi yul las rnam par dben pa dang /_sems 
[\[200b.4\]]las phyi rol du mi mthong bas rim gyis mtshan ma med pa'i gnas su 'jug pa dang /_rim gyis sa nas sar ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul la 'jug pa dang /_khams gsum sgyu ma lta bur shin tu mos shing so sor rab tu rtog pas sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin rab tu 'thob par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[200b.5\]]rang gi sems snang ba las 'das shing snang ba med pa la 'jug pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gnas pa rjes su thob par byed pa dang /_de dag gi sbyor ba skyed pa rab tu spangs pas rdo rje lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin thob nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la 'jug par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[200b.6\]]de bzhin nyid kyi rjes su 'jug pa'i sprul pa mngon par shes pa dang /_dbang dang /_snying rje dang /_thabs kyis rab tu brgyan pa/_sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du cig car 'jug pa/_mu stegs can gyi gnas su nye bar 'gro ba/_sems dang yid dang /_rnam par shes 
[\[200b.7\]]pa dang bral bas byang chub sems dpa' de gnas gyur pa'i rim gyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku 'thob par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros chen po de bas na de bzhin gshegs pa'i rjes su 'jug pa'i sku'ang 'thob par 'dod pa rnams kyis phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched dang /_

[\[201a.1\]]sems dang /_rgyu dang /_rkyen dang /_bya ba dang /_sbyor ba dang /_skye ba dang /_gnas pa dang /_'jig pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa'i spros pa rnams yongs su spang bar bya'o/_/sems tsam gyis drang por rtsol bas thog ma med pa'i dus kyi spros pa'i nyes pa 
[\[201a.2\]]la so sor rtog cing /_rnam par rtog pa'i bag chags kyi rgyu dang /_srid pa gsum po dag snang ba med pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyis skye ba med par mjal bas rang gis rig pa 'phags pa'i bgrod par phyin pa dang /_rang gi sems la dbang sgyur zhing rtsol ba med pa'i spyod pa rtogs par 
[\[201a.3\]]khong du chud pa/_gzugs sna tshogs kyi rjes su 'jug pa'i nor bu dang mtshungs pa dang /_sems can gyi sems shin tu phra ba'i rjes su 'jug cing sprul pa'i lus kyis sems kyi mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongs su dgrol bar byed pas sa rnams rim gyis mtshams sbyor ba rnam par 
[\[201a.4\]]gzhag par 'gyur ro/_/de lta bas na blo gros chen po rang gi grub pa'i mtha' la mkhas pa rab tu bsgrub par bya'o/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' blo gros chen pos yang bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das sems dang yid dang /_rnam par shes pa 
[\[201a.5\]]dang /_chos lnga dang /_rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid bshad du gsol/_'di ni sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis bgrod par bgyi ba/_rang gi sems kyis mthong ba la sogs pas dmigs par bgyi ba'i spyod yul dang 'phrod pa ma lags pa/_brjod pa'am/_
[\[201a.6\]]rigs pa dang /_de kho na nyid kyi mtshan nyid thams cad rab tu ston pa/_sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsung gi snying po'o/_/bcom ldan 'das lang ka'i grong rdal dang /_ma la ya'i ri dang /_rgya mtsho la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi slad du de 
[\[201a.7\]]bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis rab tu bsngags pa/_rgya mtsho'i dba' rlabs lta bu'i kun gzhi rnam par shes pa'i spyod yul dang /_chos kyi sku bshad du gsol/_de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' blo gros chen po la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/blo gros 
[\[201b\]]
[\[201b.1\]]chen po rkyen rnam pa bzhi'i phyir mig gi rnam par shes pa 'jug ste/_bzhi gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rang gi sems snang ba 'dzin par khong du ma chud pa dang /_thog ma med pa'i dus nas spros pa'i gnas ngan len gyi gzugs kyi bag chags la mngon par zhen pa 
[\[201b.2\]]dang /_rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhin gyi ngo bo nyid dang /_gzugs rnam pa sna tshogs kyi mtshan nyid mthong bar 'dun pa'o/_/blo gros chen po 'di ni rgyu rkyen bzhi po chu klung gi gnas dang /_kun gzhi rnam par shes pa las 'jug pa'i rnam par shes pa'i rlabs 'byung ba 
[\[201b.3\]]zhes bya'o/_/blo gros chen po mig gi rnam par shes pa ji lta ba bzhin du dbang po thams cad kyi rdul phra rab dang /_spu'i khung bu lhan cig skye ba dang /_rim gyis yul gyi rjes su skye ba yang de bzhin te/_ji ltar me long rab tu gsal ba'i nang du gzugs brnyan snang ba 
[\[201b.4\]]bzhin no/_/blo gros chen po dper na/_rlung rab tu drag pos rgya mtsho chen po'i chu rab tu skyod par byed pa de bzhin du/_phyi rol gyi yul gyi rlung gis kyang sems kyi rgya mtsho bsgul bas rnam par shes pa'i rlabs rnams rgyun mi 'chad de/_rgyu dang bya ba'i mtshan nyid tha dad pa dang /_
[\[201b.5\]]tha dad ma yin pa gcig tu 'dres pa/_las dang skye ba'i mtshan nyid la shin tu mngon par zhen pa dang /_gzugs la sogs pa'i rang gi ngo bo nyid mi rtogs pas de'i phyir rnam par shes pa'i tshogs lnga 'jug go/_/blo gros chen po rnam par shes pa'i tshogs lnga po de dag dang lhan 
[\[201b.6\]]cig pa'i rgyu la bye brag gis rnam pa'i mtshan nyid khong du chud par byed pas yid kyi rnam par shes pa'i rgyu ni de'i lus kyi rjes su 'jug pa yin par rig par bya'o/_/de dag 'di snyam du bdag cag ni phan tshun gcig gi rgyu gcig byed do snyam du mi sems te/_'on kyang 
[\[201b.7\]]rang gi sems snang ba la rnam par rtog pas mngon par zhen pa 'jug cing de dag so so nas 'jig pa'i mtshan nyid dang lhan cig tu 'jug go/_/yul gyi rnam pa'i bye brag 'byed pa ni 'di lta ste/_de dag 'jug pa'o/_/blo gros chen po ji ltar rnal 'byor pa dag 
[\[202a\]]
[\[202a.1\]]bsam gtan dang ting nge 'dzin la mnyam par bzhag pa bag chags shin du phra rab 'jug pa rnams mi shes pa de dag 'di snyam du/_bdag cag rnam par shes pa bkag nas snyoms par zhugs so snyam du sems kyang yang dag par rnam par shes pa 
[\[202a.2\]]bkag nas snyoms par zhugs pa ma yin te/_'di ltar bag chags kyi sa bon shin tu phra ba ma 'gags pa'i phyir ma 'gags pa'o/_/yul la 'jug cing 'dzin pa dang mi ldan pa'i phyir ni 'gags pa'o/_/blo gros chen po de ltar shin tu phra ba'i kun gzhi rnam par shes pa de mthar 
[\[202a.3\]]thug par rtogs pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa dang /_sa la rab tu gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams ma gtogs par gzhan nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis thob pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_shes rab kyi stobs kyis kyang

[\[202a.5\]]snang ba la rnam par rtog pa'i spros pa dang bral ba ri dang nags tshal stug po dang /_shing khrod na gnas shing rnal 'byor tha ma dang /_'bring dang /_rab tu bsgoms pa rnams kyis rang gi sems kyi rnam par rtog pa'i rgyud mthong ba dang /_zhing tshad med pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis dbang 
[\[202a.6\]]bskur ba dang /_dbang dang stobs dang /_mngon par shes pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_dge ba'i bshes gnyen sangs rgyas kyi sras kyi 'khor rnams kyang 'thob par 'gyur te/_des sems dang /_yid dang /_rnam par shes pa dang /_rang gi sems snang ba'i rang 
[\[202a.7\]]bzhin gyi spyod yul spros pa'i mtshan nyid rig cing 'khor ba srid pa'i rgya mtsho dang /_las dang /_sred pa dang /_mi shes pa la sogs pa'i rgyu las rgyal bar nus so/_/de lta bas na/_blo gros chen po rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis dge ba'i bshes gnyen rgyal ba rnams la

[\[202b.1\]]bsten par bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don de nyid yang bstan pa'i phyir/_tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/dper na rgya mtsho'i dba' rlabs rnams/_/de dag rlung drag dbang gis byung /_/rlabs chen rgya mtsho rab bskyod pas/_/rgyun ni rnam par chad

[\[204a.4\]]bya'o/_/gal te 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid 'di gsum dang ldan na rang gis rig pa 'phags pa'i ye shes mthar thug pa'i spyod yul khong du chud par 'gyur ro/_/de lta bas na blo gros chen po 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid gsum po dag la rab tu brtson

[\[205b.1\]]mngon par zhen pa nam mkha'i cha ris legs par khong du ma chud pa rnams 'di skad du/_nam mkha' ni gzugs dang bral ba'o zhes cha ris su lta ba'i rnam par rtog pa skyed par byed de/_blo gros chen po nam mkha' ni gzugs pa yin te/_gzugs kyi 'byung ba'i rjes su

[\[215a.5\]]dang bral zhing rgyu dang 'grub par byed pa dang /_bya ba dang /_las dang /_sbyor ba las skye ba ste/_'di ni 'du byed stong pa nyid ces bya'o/_/blo gros chen po 'di ltar 'du byed stong pa nyid des na phan tshun gyi rkyen gyis 'byung gi rang bzhin gyi dngos po med pas 'du byed med pa stong pa nyid ces bya'o/_/

[\[219b\]]
[\[219b.1\]]de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don de nyid yang bstan pa'i phyir tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/chos rnams thams cad mi skye ste/_/rgyu rkyen 'gag pa'ang yod ma yin/_/skye dang 'gag pa de dag la/_/rgyu dang rkyen du rtog pa skyed/_/'gags pa las ni yang skye dang /_/'brel pa'i 
[\[219b.2\]]rgyu rkyen bkag min te/_/byis pa shin tu rmongs pa yis/_/rkyen du rtog pa rab spang phyir/_/yod dang med pa rten 'brel chos/_/de dag skye ba yod ma yin/_/bag chags rmongs pas rab tu 'jug_/de las srid gsum snang ba yin/_/yang dag rkyen las skye ba med/_/'gag pa'ang yod 
[\[219b.3\]]par mi 'gyur te/_/'dus byas thams cad rab brtags na/_/nam mkha'i me tog ji bzhin no/_/gzung ba dang ni 'dzin pa yi/_/nor par mthong ba rab tu spongs/_/skyed dang skye ba ma yin te/_/rgyu rkyen dag kyang yod ma yin/_/thams cad snang ba med pas na/_/de dag kun ni brjod pa 
[\[219b.4\]]yin/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos yang bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das tshig gi rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid kyi snying po zhes bgyi ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das bdag dang /_
[\[219b.5\]]byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gzhan rnams tshig rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid kyi snying po zhes bgyi ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs shin tu rtogs pa des/_brjod pa dang /_brjod pa rnam pa gnyis kyi don rtogs par khong du chud de/_myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 
[\[219b.6\]]mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas/_brjod pa rnam pa gnyis kyi tshul gyis sems can thams cad yongs su dag par bgyid par 'gyur ro/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang ngas khyod la bshad par bya'o/_/
[\[219b.7\]]blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/_legs so/_/bcom ldan 'das zhes bka' mnyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/blo gros chen po tshig gi rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid ni rnam pa bzhi yod de/_'di lta ste/_mtshan nyid kyi tshig 
[\[220a\]]
[\[220a.1\]]dang /_rmi lam gyi tshig dang /_gnas ngan len gyi rnam par rtog pa la mngon par zhen pa'i tshig dang /_thog ma med pa'i dus kyi rnam par rtog pa'i tshig go/_/de la mtshan nyid kyi tshig ni rang gi rnam par rtog pa'i gzugs kyi mtshan nyid la mngon par zhen pa las 'byung ngo /_/
[\[220a.2\]]rmi lam gyi tshig ni sngon myong ba'i yul rjes su dran pa dang /_sad nas yul dngos po med pa las 'byung ngo /_/gnas ngan len gyi rnam par rtog pa las mngon par zhen pa'i tshig ni sngon dgras byas pa'i las rjes su dran pa las 'byung ngo /_/thog ma med pa'i dus kyi rnam par rtog pa'i tshig ni thog ma 
[\[220a.3\]]med pa'i dus kyi rnam par spros pa la mngon par zhen pa'i gnas ngan len gyi rang gi sa bon gyi bag chags las 'byung ste/_'di ni tshig gi rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid rnam pa bzhi zhes bya'o/_/de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos don de nyid kyi phyir bcom 
[\[220a.4\]]ldan 'das la gsol ba btab pa/_bcom ldan 'das da dung yang rnam par rtog pa'i mngon pa'i spyod yul bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das gang nas ci slad/_ji ltar rgyu gang gis sems can rnams kyi rnam par rtog pa'i tshig 'byung ba lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_
[\[220a.5\]]blo gros chen po mgo dang /_brang dang /_mgul pa dang /_sna dang /_mchu dang /_lce dang /_rkan dang /_so rnams 'dus pa las sgra skad 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das tshig dang /_rnam par rtog pa tha dad pa'am/_'on te gcig pa zhig 
[\[220a.6\]]lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po tshig dang /_rnam par rtog pa ni tha dad pa yang ma yin/_tha mi dad pa yang ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_de'i rgyu las byung ba'i mtshan nyid kyi phyir ro/_/blo gros chen po gal te tshig dang /_rnam par rtog 
[\[220a.7\]]pa tha dad pa zhig yin na ni rnam par rtog pa rgyu yin par mi 'gyur ro/_/ci ste tha mi dad pa zhig yin na ni tshig gi don gsal bar byed par yang mi 'gyur ba zhig na/_gsal bar yang byed de/_de bas na tha dad pa yang ma yin/_tha mi dad pa yang ma yin no/_/blo gros chen pos yang bcom 
[\[220b\]]
[\[220b.1\]]ldan 'das la gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das tshig nyid don dam pa lags sam/_'on te tshig gis brjod par bgyi ba de don dam pa lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po tshig kyang don dam pa ma yin la/_tshig gis gang brjod par bya ba de yang don dam pa 
[\[220b.2\]]ma yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di ltar don dam pa ni 'phags pa'i bden pa yin te/_tshig gis don dam pa la 'jug kyang tshig 'di nyid ni don dam pa ma yin no/_/don dam pa ni 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i ye shes kyis rtogs par bya ba yin gyi/_tshig gi rnam par rtog pa'i blo'i 
[\[220b.3\]]spyod yul ni ma yin pas/_de'i phyir tshig gi rnam par rtog pas don dam pa gsal bar mi byed do/_/de la tshig ni skye ba dang 'jig pa dag gis rab tu g.yo ba/_phan tshun gyi rgyu dang rkyen las byung ba'o/_/gal te phan tshun gyi rgyu dang rkyen las byung ba yin na/_des ni don dam pa gsal bar mi byed 
[\[220b.4\]]do/_/blo gros chen po bdag dang gzhan dang /_gnyi ga'i dngos po med pa'i phyir/_tshig gi mtshan nyid kyis don dam pa gsal bar mi byed do/_/gzhan blo gros chen po rang gi sems snang ba tsam gyi rjes su 'jug pa'i phyir mtshan ma rnam pa sna tshogs dang /_phyi rol gyi dngos po dngos po med pas/_
[\[220b.5\]]tshig gi rnam par rtog pas ni don dam pa gsal bar mi byed do/_/de lta bas na blo gros chen po tshig gi rnam par rtog pa rnams kyi mtshan nyid yongs su spang bar bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don de nyid yang bstan pa'i phyir tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/dngos po kun gyi rang bzhin 
[\[220b.6\]]med/_/tshig kyang yod pa ma yin te/_/zab mo stong nyid stong pa'i don/_/byis pa rnams kyis rtogs mi nus/_/dngos po thams cad rang bzhin dang /_/tshig gi chos ni mig yor 'dra/_/rang rig ye shes yang dag mtha'/_/bu rnams la ni ngas bshad do/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[220b.7\]]sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos yang bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das mchis pa dang /_ma mchis pa dang /_gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_gnyi ga dang /_gnyi ga ma lags pa dang /_mchis pa yang ma lags/_ma mchis pa yang ma lags 
[\[221a\]]
[\[221a.1\]]pa dang /_rtag pa dang /_mi rtag pa spangs pa/_mu stegs can thams cad kyis bgrod par bgyi ba ma lags pa/_so so rang gis rig pa 'phags pa'i ye shes kyis bgrod par bgyi ba/_kun brtags pa'i rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid dang bral ba/_don dam pa de kho na'i don 
[\[221a.2\]]la 'jug pa/_sa rnams kyi mtshams sbyor zhing /_rim gyis gong nas gong du khyad par can du 'gyur ba rnam par dag pa'i mtshan nyid dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la rjes su 'jug pa'i mtshan nyid la rtsol ba ma mchis par sngon gyi smon lam gyis gzugs sna tshogs kyi yid bzhin gyi 
[\[221a.3\]]nor bu ltar spyod yul mtha' yas pa'i mtshan nyid kyi rnam pa/_rang gi sems snang ba'i tshul bye brag gi mtshan nyid kyi chos thams cad dang /_ci nas kyang bdag dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gzhan rnams 'di lta bu la sogs pa kun brtags pa'i rang bzhin dang /_rang 
[\[221a.4\]]dang spyi'i mtshan nyid kyi lta ba rnams spangs te/_myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas/_sems can thams cad kyi bde ba phun sum tshogs pa yongs su rdzogs par bgyi ba bshad du gsol/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal 
[\[221a.5\]]pa/_blo gros chen po legs so legs so/_/khyod nga la don 'di 'dri ba ni skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /_skye bo mang po la phan pa dang /_lha dang mi'i 'jig rten thams cad la snying brtse ba'i phyir zhugs pa ste/_blo gros chen po de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang /_ngas 
[\[221a.6\]]khyod la rab tu dbye zhing bshad par bya'o/_/blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la/_bcom ldan 'das legs so zhes gsol nas/_bka' mnyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/blo gros chen po byis pa so so'i skye bo rang gi sems snang ba tsam du khong du ma chud 
[\[221a.7\]]pa dang /_nang dang phyi rol gyi dngos por 'dzin pas/_gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_gnyi ga dang /_gnyi ga ma yin pa dang /_yod pa dang /_med pa dang /_yod pa yang ma yin/_med pa yang ma yin pa dang /_rtag pa dang mi rtag pa'i rang bzhin gyi bag chags kyi rgyu la brten nas/_

[\[221b.1\]]mngon par zhen pas rnam par rtog go/_/dper na [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]skom pas rab tu gdungs pa rnams dpyid kyi smig rgyu la chu nyid du rnam par rtog pa'i nor pas rab tu rgyug ste/_chu ma yin par yongs su mi shes so/_/de bzhin du byis pa so so'i skye bo thog ma med pa'i dus kyis spros pa'i rnam par rtog pas 
[\[221b.2\]]yongs su bsgos pa'i dug gsum gyi mes sems rab tu gdungs pa/_gzugs kyi yul la shin tu mos pa/_skye ba dang /_gnas pa dang /_'jig par lta ba/_phyi nang gi dngos por yongs su 'dzin pa dag kyang /_gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_gnyi ga dang /_gnyi ga ma yin pa dang /_

[\[221b.4\]]'byung ste/_thog ma med pa'i dus nas/_bag chags la mngon par zhen pa'i 'du shes kyis snang yang /_de ni grong khyer yang ma yin/_grong khyer ma yin pa yang ma yin no/_/de bzhin du mu stegs can thog ma med pa'i dus nas spros pa'i bag chags la mngon par zhen pa rnams gcig pa

[\[222a.1\]]ldan 'das kyis blo gros chen po la bka' stsal pa/_de bzhin du byis pa so so'i skye bo lta ba ngan pas rab tu zin pa/_mu stegs kyi blo can dag rang gi sems snang ba'i dngos po rmi lam lta bur ma shes pas/_gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_gnyi ga dang /_gnyi ga ma yin pa dang /_yod

[\[223a.1\]]bstan pa ni ma yin no/_/gzhan yang bstan pa rnams ni byis pa so so'i skye bo lta ba'i bsam pa can gyi dbang gis byung ba yin gyi/_logs shig rnam par 'jog pa'i tshul rang bzhin gyi chos/_'phags pa'i ye shes so so rang gis rig pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi bde ba la gnas pa thob pa rab tu dbye zhing ston

[\[224a.1\]]sprin dang rmi lam glog lta bur/_/rab tu rtog na rnam grol zhing /_/rgyun gsum dag ni gtan du spongs/_/'di la byed pa gang yang med/_/smig rgyu nam mkha' ji bzhin te/_/de ltar chos rnams rab shes na/_/shes par bya ba ci yang med/_/bstan pa dag ni ming tsam ste/_/de la mtshan 
[\[224a.2\]]nyid yod ma yin/_/gang la rnam rtog skyed pa yi/_/phung po'i 'du byed skra shad 'dra/_/ri mo skra shad 'dzings pa dang /_/sgyu ma rmi lam dri za'i grong /_/mgal me'i 'khor lo'i smig rgyu dag_/_med kyang sems can rnams la snang /_/rtag dang mi rtag gcig tha dad/_/gnyi ga gnyis min de bzhin te/_/thog ma med kyi nyes 
[\[224a.3\]]'brel bas/_/byis pa mos pas rab tu rtog_/me long chu dang mig rnams dang /_/yid bzhin nor bu rin po che/_/de na gzugs brnyan snang mod kyi/_/de ni yang dag yod pa min/_/dngos por snang ba thams cad ni/_/ri mo dang ni smig rgyu bzhin/_/rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs snang yang /_/rmi lam 'dra ste yod ma yin/_/gzhan 
[\[224a.4\]]yang blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi bstan pa'i chos ni mu bzhi dang bral ba yin te/_'di lta ste/_gcig pa dang /_tha dad pa dang /_gnyi ga dang /_gnyi ga ma yin pa dang /_yod pa dang /_med pa dang /_yod pa yang ma yin/_med pa yang ma yin pa dang /_rtag pa dang /_mi rtag pa spangs pa/_yod pa dang /_
[\[224a.5\]]med pa'i sgro 'dogs pa dang /_skur pa las rnam par grol ba'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos bstan pa ni yang dag pa'i bden pa dang /_rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba dang /_lam dang /_'gog pa dang /_rnam par thar pa rab tu 'byed cing sdud par byed pas de dag sngon du btang ba yin gyi/_ngo bo nyid dang /_dbang

[\[224b.1\]]bsam gtan no/_/de la byis pa nye bar spyod pa'i bsam gtan gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor pa rnams gang zag la bdag med pa dang /_dngos po'i rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid keng rus dang /_mi rtag pa dang /_sdug bsngal ba dang /_mi sdug 
[\[224b.2\]]pa'i mtshan nyid la mngon par zhen pa sngon du btang ste/_'di ni mtshan nyid 'di lta bu yin te/_gzhan du ma yin no zhes brtags nas snga ma las gong nas gong du khyad par can du 'gyur te/_'du shes mi 'gog pa gang yin pa de ni byis pa nye bar spyod pa'i bsam gtan zhes bya'o/_/don rab tu 'byed pa'i 
[\[224b.3\]]bsam gtan gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_gang zag la bdag med pa dang /_rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid dang /_mu stegs can gyi bdag dang /_gzhan dang /_gnyi ga dngos po med par gyur pa la chos bdag med par brtags nas/_sa de dag gi mtshan nyid kyi don gong nas gong du khyad par can du 'gyur ba ni 
[\[224b.4\]]don rab tu 'byed pa'i bsam gtan zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin nyid la dmigs pa'i bsam gtan gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rnam par rtog pas bdag med pa gnyis su rnam par rtog pa yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin gyi gnas la rnam par rtog pa mi skyed pa ni de bzhin nyid la dmigs pa'i bsam gtan zhes 
[\[224b.5\]]bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsam gtan gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa'i rnam pa la 'jug pa so so rang gis rig pa 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid gsum gyi bde ba la gnas nas/_sems can gyi don bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i bya ba byed pa ni de bzhin

[\[224b.7\]]pa'i dge bsam gtan/_/rnal 'byor can gyis brtson byas na/_/nyi ma zla ba'i dbyibs 'dra dang /_/pad ma dang ni g.yang sa dang /_/nam mkha' dang ni me zad ltar/_/rnal 'byor pas ni gang rtog pa/_/mtshan ma sna tshogs de dag ni/_/mu stegs lam 'byed bsam gtan te/_/nyan thos dang ni rang rgyal 
[\[225a\]]
[\[225a.1\]]gyi/_/spyod yul du yang ltung bar byed/_/'di dag thams cad rab spangs na/_/de ni snang ba med pa yin/_/zhing rnams kun gyi sangs rgyas kyi/_/bsam gyis mi khyab phyag gis ni/_/de yi mgo la nyug mdzad de/_/de bzhin nyid kyi rjes zhugs brtags/_/de

[\[225a.3\]]po rang bzhin thams cad kyi bag chags kyi kun gzhi dang /_yid dang /_yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang /_lta ba'i bag chags rab tu gyur pa ni mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bya ste/_sangs rgyas rnams dang /_nga'i mya ngan las 'das pa rang bzhin stong pa nyid kyi dngos po'i spyod yul lo/_/gzhan 
[\[225a.4\]]yang blo gros chen po mya ngan las 'das pa ni 'phags pa so so rang gis rig pa'i ye shes kyi spyod yul te/_rtag pa dang /_chad par rnam par rtog pa dang /_dngos po dang /_dngos po med par rnam par spangs pa'o/_/ji ltar rtag pa ma yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid du rnam

[\[225b.1\]]gros chen po nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas kyi mya ngan las 'das pa ni rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid khong du chud pa dang /_yul gyi 'du 'dzi med pa dang /_phyin ci log par mthong bas rnam par rtog pa mi skye ba ste/_de dag gis de la mya ngan las 'das pa'i blor 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang blo

[\[242a\]]
[\[242a.1\]]dang /_dpe byad bzang po'i skur mtshungs shing bye brag med de/_'gro ba'i bye brag sems can de dang de dag gdul ba'i phyir gzugs tha dad pa sna tshogs ston pa ni ma gtogs te/_de ni sku mtshungs pa zhes bya'o/_/de la chos mtshungs pa gang zhe na/_
[\[242a.2\]]'di lta ste/_nga dang sangs rgyas de dag byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos sum cu rtsa bdun thugs su chud de/_sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams dang /_sgrib pa med pa'i ye shes mdor bsdus te bstan pa de ni mtshungs pa rnam pa bzhi zhes bya ste/_de lta bas na de bzhin gshegs pa 
[\[242a.3\]]dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams 'khor gyi nang du de skad ces gsung ngo /_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don de nyid yang bstan pa'i phyir tshigs su bcad pa 'di gsungs so/_/'od srung dang ni 'khor ba 'jig_/gser thub kyang ni 
[\[242a.4\]]nga yin zhes/_/mtshungs pa bzhi ni mnyam gyur pas/_/rgyal ba'i sras la ngas bshad do/_/blo gros chen pos yang bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/_ji skad du bcom ldan 'das kyis nga ni gang gi nub mo mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa nas gang gi nub mo yongs 
[\[242a.5\]]su mya ngan las 'da' bar 'gyur ba de'i bar du yi ge gcig kyang ma bshad/_mi 'chad 'chad par mi 'gyur te/_bshad pa med pa ni sangs rgyas kyis bshad pa'o zhes gsungs na/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par 
[\[242a.6\]]rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ci'i rgyus bshad pa med pa ni sangs rgyas kyis bshad pa'o zhes gsungs/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po ngas chos rnam pa gnyis la dgongs nas de skad ces smras so/_/chos gnyis gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_
[\[242a.7\]]rang gis mngon par rtogs pa'i chos dang /_ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa'i chos te/_'di ni chos gnyis zhes bya'o/_/chos gnyis po de las dgongs nas de'i phyir ngas de skad ces bshad do/_/de la rang gis mngon par rtogs pa'i chos gang zhe na/_de bzhin gshegs 
[\[242b\]]
[\[242b.1\]]pa de dag gi gang thugs su chud pa de bzhin du ngas kyang rang gis mngon par rtogs pa'i chos mthar phyin pa'i spyod yul tshig gi rnam par rtog pa dang bral ba/_yi ge'i tshul rnam pa gnyis las rnam par grol ba/_lhag chad med par thugs su chud pa'o/_/de la 
[\[242b.2\]]ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa'i chos gang zhe na/_'di lta ste/_sngon gyi 'phags pa'i lam rnying pa gser dang /_dngul la sogs pa'i ngo bo nyid bzhin du chos kyi dbyings rtag par gnas pa yang de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten du byung yang rung /_'jig rten du ma 
[\[242b.3\]]byung yang rung ste/_chos kyi dbyings rtag par gnas pa ni ji ltar grong khyer de dag du 'gro ba'i lam bzhin du 'dug pa'o/_/blo gros chen po dper na/_skyes bu la la zhig 'brog dgon pa na 'khyam pa las grong khyer rnying par 'gro ba'i lam mnyam zhing ma gol ba zhig 
[\[242b.4\]]mthong nas/_grong khyer der zhugs te yid bzhin gyi bde ba rjes su myong na blo gros chen po de ji snyam du sems/_lam de dang /_grong khyer na yod pa'i bde ba sna tshogs de dag des byas pa yin nam/_gsol pa/_de ma lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' 
[\[242b.5\]]stsal pa/_blo gros chen po nga dang 'das pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa dag gis thugs su chud pa'i chos kyi dbyings mi 'gyur bar gnas pa yang de bzhin te/_des na de'i phyir ngas 'di skad du gang gi nub mo ngas mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa nas gang gi nub 
[\[242b.6\]]mo yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar 'gyur ba de'i bar du yi ge gcig kyang ma bshad/_mi 'chad 'chad par mi 'gyur ro/_/zhes smras so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don de nyid yang bstan pa'i phyir tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/gang gi nub mo 
[\[242b.7\]]mngon sangs rgyas/_/gang gi nub mo mya ngan 'das/_/de dag gnyis kyi bar du yang /_/nga yis cung zad bshad pa med/_/rang gis mngon rtogs chos gnas pas/_/de phyir ngas ni 'di skad bshad/_/nga dang sangs rgyas de dag la/_/bye brag gyur pa ci yang med/_/
[\[243a\]]
[\[243a.1\]]de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba btab pa/_bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad kyi mchis pa dang /_ma mchis pa'i mtshan nyid bshad du gsol/_/des na bdag dang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
[\[243a.2\]]chen po gzhan rnams mchis pa dang ma mchis pa spangs nas myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur ro/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid 
[\[243a.3\]]la zung shig dang ngas khyod la bshad do/_/blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la legs so zhes gsol nas bka' mnyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po 'jig rten 'di dag ni gnyis la gnas so/_/'di lta ste/_yod pa 
[\[243a.4\]]la gnas pa dang /_dngos po rnams dngos po med pa la mos pa'i lta bar ltung ba ste/_'byung ba ma yin pa la 'byung bar 'du shes pa'o/_/de la blo gros chen po ji ltar na 'jig rten pa dag yod pa la gnas pa yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_'jig rten ni rgyu dang rkyen rnams yod 
[\[243a.5\]]na skye'i med pa las ma yin pas/_yod pa las skye ste/_med pa las skye ba ni ma yin no zhes blo gros chen po des de skad du smra na/_'jig rten rgyu med par smra ba yin no/_/blo gros chen po ji ltar na 'jig rten med pa las gnas pa yin zhe na/_'di lta ste/_'dod chags 
[\[243a.6\]]dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi dngos por khas blang ba byas la/_de'i 'og tu 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi dngos po med par rnam par rtog cing mngon par zhen pa'o/_/blo gros chen po dngos po yod par khas mi len pa ni dngos po'i mtshan nyid rab 
[\[243a.7\]]tu dben pa'i phyir 'di ltar de bzhin gshegs pa dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas rnams 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi dngos po la yod ces bya ba dang /_med ces bya bar khas len par mi byed do/_/blo gros chen po de la ji ltar na rnam par 
[\[243b\]]
[\[243b.1\]]'jig par byed pa yin/_blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das gang des 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi dngos po khas blangs la slad kyis ni khas mi len pa dag lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal 
[\[243b.2\]]pa/_blo gros chen po legs so legs so/_/gang khyod kyis de ltar go ba ni legs so/_/blo gros chen po de ni 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi dngos po dngos po med pa 'ba' zhig rnam par 'jig par byed pa ma yin gyi nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas 
[\[243b.3\]]rnams kyang rnam par 'jig pa yin no/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'di lta ste/_nang dang phyi rol mi dmigs pa'i phyir dang nyon mongs pa rnams tha dad pa dang /_tha dad pa ma yin pa'i phyir ro/_/blo gros chen po 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug rnams ni 
[\[243b.4\]]nang dang phyi rol na yang mi dmigs te/_'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug gi dngos po lus med pa'i phyir dang /_khas len pa med pa'i phyir ro/_/sangs rgyas dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas rnams ni rnam par 'jig par byed pa ma yin te/_
[\[243b.5\]]sangs rgyas dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas de dag ni rang bzhin gyis rnam par grol ba'i phyir dang /_'ching ba dang bcing ba'i rgyu dngos po med pa'i phyir ro/_/blo gros chen po bcing bar bya ba yod na bcing ba yang yod de/_bcing ba'i rgyu yin pa'i phyir ro/_/
[\[243b.6\]]blo gros chen po de skad du rnam par 'jig par byed pa smra ba 'di yang med pa'i mtshan nyid ces bya'o/_/blo gros chen po ngas de la dgongs nas gang zag tu lta ba ri rab tsam yod pa ni bla'i/_mngon pa'i nga rgyal can gyi stong pa nyid du lta ba ni ma yin no zhes bshad de/_
[\[243b.7\]]blo gros chen po med pa'i mngon pa'i nga rgyal can ni rnam par 'jig par byed pa yin no/_/rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid du ltung ba'i bsam pa can dag rang gi sems snang ba tsam du mi shes pas phyi rol gyi dngos po rnams mi rtag cing skad cig ma gcig nas gcig tu rnam par 
[\[244a\]]
[\[244a.1\]]'jig par mthong nas/_phung po dang /_khams dang /_skye mched rnams ni rgyun mi 'chad cing rnam par 'gyur ba dang /_yi ge'i mtshan nyid dang bral bar rnam par rtog na yang rnam par 'jig par byed pa yin no/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don 
[\[244a.2\]]de nyid yang bstan pa'i phyir tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/yod dang med pa mtha' gnyis te/_/ci tsam sems kyi spyod yul rnams/_/spyod yul de dag rab spangs na/_/sems mnyam rab dben zhi bar 'gyur/_/yul gyi dngos la 'dzin med pas/_/'gog 
[\[244a.3\]]pa med pa ma yin te/_/yod pa'i dngos rnams de bzhin nyid/_/'phags pa'i spyod yul ji bzhin no/_/rgyu med pa las skye 'gyur zhing /_/byung nas de ni 'gag par 'gyur/_/rgyu rkyen yod dang med ces pa/_/nga yi bstan la de mi gnas/_/mu stegs can dang sangs rgyas 
[\[244a.4\]]dang /_/nga dang gzhan gyis byas min te/_/rgyu rkyen 'dus las byung yin na/_/ji lta bur na med par 'gyur/_/rgyu rkyen yod pa sus bsags na/_/med pa zhes ni rjod par byed/_/log lta smra ba chos skye ba/_/rnam par rtog pas yod med brtags/_/gang gis 
[\[244a.5\]]cung zad skye med dang /_/'gag pa med par rab shes pa/_/'jig rten dben par mthong bas na/_/yod med gnyis las rnam par 'grol/_/de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/
[\[244a.6\]]bcom ldan 'das bdag dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhan rnams la grub pa'i mtha'i tshul gyi mtshan nyid bshad du gsol/_grub pa'i mtha'i tshul gyi mtshan nyid rab tu dbye ba la mkhas par gyur na bdag dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams de'i mtshan nyid rtogs 
[\[244a.7\]]par 'gyur te/_de'i mtshan nyid rtogs nas kyang myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya zhing rtog pa'i mtshan nyid dang /_bdud dang mu stegs can gyi rjes su 'brang bar mi 'gyur ro/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' 
[\[244b\]]
[\[244b.1\]]stsal pa/_blo gros chen po de'i phyir shin du legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang /_ngas khyod la bshad do/_/blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la de bzhin 'tshal lo zhes bka' mnyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen 
[\[244b.2\]]po nyan thos dang /_rang sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi grub pa'i mtha'i tshul gyi mtshan nyid ni rnam pa gnyis te 'di lta ste/_grub pa'i mtha'i tshul dang /_bshad pa'i tshul lo/_/de la blo gros chen po grub pa'i mtha'i tshul ni 'di lta ste/_so so rang 
[\[244b.3\]]gis rig pa'i bye brag gi mtshan nyid thob pa la dmigs pa/_tshig dang yi ge'i rnam par rtog pa dang bral ba zag pa med pa'i dbyings su 'jug pa/_rang gis rtogs pa'i sa'i rang gi mtshan nyid yang dag pa ma yin pa/_rtog pa can gyi 'du shes thams cad spangs pa/_
[\[244b.4\]]mu stegs can dang /_bdud thams cad btul nas so so rang gis rig pa'i tshul la dmigs pa'i 'od kyis rnam par mdzes pa ni/_grub pa'i mtha'i tshul gyi mtshan nyid ces bya'o/_/de la bshad pa'i tshul gyi mtshan nyid gang zhe na 'di lta ste/_gsung rab yan lag rnam 
[\[244b.5\]]pa dgu sna tshogs su bshad pa yin te/_tha dad pa dang /_tha dad pa ma yin pa dang /_yod pa dang /_med pa la sogs pa'i mtshan nyid spangs pa thabs mkhas pas sems can gyi mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du chos bstan nas rnam par grol ba 'thob par byed pa de ni bshad pa'i 
[\[244b.6\]]tshul gyi mtshan nyid ces bya'o/_/blo gros chen po de lta bas na khyod dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhan dag gis de la brtson par bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don de nyid yang bstan pa'i phyir tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/grub pa'i mtha' dang bshad 
[\[244b.7\]]tshul ni/_/so so rang rig bstan pa ste/_/gang gis mthong zhing rab 'byed na/_/de dag rtog pa'i dbang mi 'gro/_/ji ltar byis pa rtog pa bzhin/_/yang dag dngos po yod min na/_/ci phyir rtog pa can rnams kyis/_/dngos po med la thar par 'dod/_/'dus byas so sor

[\[245a.1\]]rtog pa'i tshe/_/skye 'jig la sogs 'brel mthong ba/_/lta ba gnyis po rgyas par byed/_/phyin log shes pa med pa yin/_/gcig pa de ni yang dag bden/_/nyes med de ni mya ngan 'das/_/'jig rten rnam rtog rab brtags na/_/sgyu ma rmi lam

[\[245a.3\]]'das yang dag pa ma lags pa yongs su rtog pa'i mtshan nyid bdag la bshad du gsol/_yang dag pa ma lags pa'i yongs su rtog pa rnams ji ltar 'byung bar 'gyur/_ji lta bu la yang dag pa ma lags pa'i yongs su rtog pa zhes bgyi/_chos gang zhig la yang 
[\[245a.4\]]dag pa ma lags pa yongs su rtog lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po legs so legs so/_/de bzhin gshegs pa la de lta bu'i don 'dri bar sems pa ni skye bo mang po la phan pa dang /_skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /_'jig rten la snying 
[\[245a.5\]]brtse ba dang /_lha dang /_mi phal po che'i don dang /_phan pa dang bde ba'i phyir zhugs pa yin te/_de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang /_ngas khyod la bshad do/_/blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/_legs so legs so/_/
[\[245a.6\]]bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du 'tshal lo zhes bka' mnyan pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po don rnam pa tha dad pa sna tshogs dang /_yang dag pa ma yin pa'i rnam par rtog pa sna tshogs las mngon par zhen pa'i rnam par rtog pa 'byung 
[\[245a.7\]]ste/_blo gros chen po gzung ba dang /_'dzin pa la mngon par zhen pa dang /_rang gi sems snang ba tsam du ma rtogs pa dang /_yod pa dang med pa'i lta bar ltung ba/_mu stegs can gyi lta ba shin tu rgyas par byed pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa'i bag chags kyis phyi rol 
[\[245b\]]
[\[245b.1\]]gyi don sna tshogs la mngon par zhen pa dang /_sems dang sems las byung ba'i rnam par rtog pa ni bdag dang bdag gir mngon par zhen pa las 'byung ngo /_/blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/_bcom ldan 'das gal te don rnam pa tha dad pa sna

[\[245b.3\]]lhung ba/_mu stegs can gyi lta ba rgyas par bgyid pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa'i bag chags kyis phyi rol gyi don sna tshogs la mngon par zhen pa dang /_sems dang sems las byung ba'i rnam par rtog pa dag_/bdag dang bdag gir mngon par zhen pa las 'byung 
[\[245b.4\]]ba zhig lags na/_bcom ldan 'das de lta na ni phyi rol gyi don sna tshogs kyi mtshan nyid dang /_mchis pa dang ma mchis par lhung ba'i mtshan nyid dngos po dang /_dngos po ma mchis pa dang bral ba dang /_lta ba dang bral ba'i mtshan nyid las ji ltar bzlog pa de bzhin du

[\[246a.1\]]med pa'i phyir dang /_rang gi sems snang ba tsam du rtogs pa'i phyir rnam par rtog pa mi skye'o/_/blo gros chen po ngas ni gzhan byis pa so so'i skye bo rang gi sems sna tshogs kyi rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid kyi phyir las kyi dngos po mngon du gyur pas

[\[246a.3\]]cing rnam par rtog pa/_rang gi sems snang ba tsam du rtogs pas lus dang sems shin tu gyur te/_sa thams cad la mthar phyin par mkhas shing de bzhin gshegs pa dag gi so so rang gis rig pa'i spyod yul la 'jug pa dang /_chos lnga dang /_rang bzhin dang /_

[\[246a.7\]]pa rnams ni ci phyir rtog_/yod pa ma yin med pa'ang min/_/de bzhin yod med gnyi ga min/_/de ltar 'jig rten rab mthong na/_/sems gyur bdag med pa yang 'thob/_/dngos po thams cad mi skye ste/_/rkyen rnams las ni byung ba'i phyir/_/thams cad rkyen gyis

[\[246b.1\]]byas pa yin/_/byas pa rnams ni bdag yod min/_/dngos pos dngos po mi skyed de/_/dngos po gnyis yod skyon phyir dang /_/dngos po gnyis med skyon gyi phyir/_/dngos po rnams ni gnyis su med/_/'dus byas chos rnams rab brtags te/_/dmigs byed dmigs

[\[246b.6\]]de rnam par grol zhes pa/_/sems tsam du yang ngas bshad do/_/de bzhin nyid dang yang dag mtha'/_/mya ngan 'das dang chos kyi dbyings/_/sna tshogs yid kyi lus rnams ni/_/sems tsam du yang ngas bshad do/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' blo gros chen

[\[252a.1\]]gsungs shing 'phags pa'i rang bzhin gyi dngos po bka' stsal/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_blo gros chen po nga ni stong pa'i chos dngos po med par bshad pa yang ma yin/_yod pa'i lta bar lhung bas 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi rang

[\[266a.1\]]bshad pa'i skye ba med pa dang /_'gag pa med pa ni mu stegs can rnams kyi skye ba med pa dang 'gag pa med pa dang mtshungs pa ma yin te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_mu stegs can de dag ni dngos po'i rang bzhin yod pas ma skyes pa dang /_mi 'gyur ba'i mtshan nyid mi 'thob kyi nga 
[\[266a.2\]]ni de ltar yod pa dang /_med pa'i phyogs su lhung ba ma yin no/_/blo gros chen po nga ni yod pa dang med pa'i phyogs dang bral ba/_skye ba dang 'jig pa dang bral ba/_dngos po yang ma yin/_dngos po med pa yang ma yin te/_sgyu ma dang /_rmi lam gyi gzugs sna tshogs lta bur snang ba'i phyir dngos

[\[266b.1\]]pa so so'i skye bo mi mkhas pa rnams ni yang dag pa ma yin par lhung bas skye ba dang 'gag pa'i rnam par rtog pa 'byung gi 'phags pa rnams ni ma yin no/_/de la yang dag pa ma yin pa zhes bya ba ni dngos po'i rang bzhin brtags pa de bzhin du yang ma yin/_gzhan du yang ma yin no/_/gal

[\[283a.7\]]bya'o/_/'di ltar srog gcod pa ni gzugs mthong ma thag tu mngon par chags pa'i sems skyed par byed pa'i phyir sha mi za'o/_/gang sha za ba de dag la ni lha rnams kyis spong bar byed pa'i phyir sha bza' bar mi bya'o/_/kha'i dri mi zhim par 'gyur ba'i phyir yang sha bza' bar mi bya'o/_/rmi lam ngan pa mang 
[\[283b\]]
[\[283b.1\]]ba'i phyir sha bza' bar mi bya'o/_/dgon pa'i gnas nags tshal dag na gnas pa'i tshe stag dang spyang ki la sogs pas dri tshor bar 'gyur ba'i phyir sha bza' bar mi bya'o/_/zas kyi tshod mi zin par byed pa'i phyir sha bza' bar mi bya'o/_/rnal 'byor pa rnams yongs su skyo ba'i sems skye 
[\[283b.2\]]bar mi 'gyur ba'i phyir sha bza' bar mi bya'o/_/ngas rtag tu ji tsam du bza' ba dang btung ba rnams la bu'i sha'i 'du shes dang /_sman gyi 'du shes bya'o zhes bshad pa'i phyir sha bza' bar mi bya ste/_ngas sha bza' bar gnang ngo zhes zer na de ni gnas med do/_/gzhan yang 
[\[283b.3\]]blo gros chen po sngon 'das pa'i dus na rgyal po seng ge 'bangs bzang zhes bya ba zhig byung ste/_sha sna tshogs la za zhing mi sha yang zos par gyur pas blon po dang /_gnyen dang /_gnyen 'dab kyi skye bo'i tshogs rnams log nas sngon gyi dbang thang rnams kyang bcad pas 
[\[283b.4\]]nyon mongs par gyur to/_/de lta bas na sha za ba la nyes dmigs 'di lta bu yod pa'i phyir sha bza' bar mi bya'o/_/gzhan yang blo gros chen po de la srog gcod pa dag ni khe 'dod pa'i phyir srog bcad nas 'tshong bar byed do/_/gang blun po sha za ba de dag ni rin gyis 
[\[283b.5\]]sha rnams nyo bar byed do/_/srog gcod par byed pa de dag khe 'dod pa'i phyir lcags kyu dang rgya rnams kyis srog chags nam mkha' la rgyu ba dang /_chu la gnas pa dang /_sa la 'gro ba dag rnam pa du mas gsod cing khe tshol bar byed do/_/blo gros chen po 
[\[283b.6\]]byed du ma bcug pa dang /_ma btsal ba dang /_'du shes med pa zhes bya ba'i sha gang yang med pas de'i phyir sha bza' bar mi bya'o/_/blo gros chen po ngas sngar sha rnam pa lnga dang bcu bkag la mdo sde 'dir ni rnam pa thams cad dang dus thams cad du thabs kyis gnang ba 
[\[283b.7\]]thams cad kyang bkag go/_/blo gros chen po de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la ni bza' bar bya ba yang med na nya'i sha za ba lta ci smos te/_gzhan yang byed du mi 'jug go/_/thugs rje chen po sngon du 'gro ba'i phyir 
[\[284a\]]
[\[284a.1\]]sems can thams cad bu gcig pa dang 'dra bar gzigs te/_de'i phyir bu'i sha bza' bar mi gnang ba yin no/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don de nyid yang bstan pa'i phyir tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so/_/sngon chad gnyen bshes 
[\[284a.2\]]byas phyir dang /_/ngan pa mi gtsang 'dres pa dang /_/khu chu khrag las byung ba dang /_/dri tshor thams cad 'jigs skrag pas/_/sha rnams dang ni btsong dag dang /_/ki'u dang byis ku la sogs dang /_/bag med sna tshogs 'gyur ba'i chang /_/rnal 'byor 
[\[284a.3\]]pas ni rtag tu spang /_/'bru mar dag kyang spang bya ste/_/bug phug pa yi khri yang spang /_/srog chags dag ni phra mo rnams/_/de na rab tu 'jigs phyir ro/_/zas las bag med skye bar 'gyur/_/bag med pa las rtog pa skye/_/rtog pa las ni 'dod 
[\[284a.4\]]chags skye/_/de yi phyir na bza' mi bya/_/zas kyi dbang gis 'dod chags skye/_/'dod chags kyis ni sems rmongs byed/_/rmongs pas srid pa'i 'dod pa skyed/_/'khor ba las ni grol mi 'gyur/_/khe yi phyir ni sems can gsod/_/sha yi phyir 
[\[284a.5\]]ni nor sbyin byed/_/gnyi ga sdig pa'i las can pas/_/shi nas 'o dod 'bod par ltung /_/bcol tshol 'du shes de med na/_/rnam gsum dag pa'i sha yang med/_/rgyu med las ni yod min pas/_/de yi phyir na bza' mi bya/_/rnal 'byor pa ni de dag gis/_/
[\[284a.6\]]des na thams cad spang byar bya/_/phyogs bcu sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/_/thams cad rab tu smod par 'gyur/_/gcig la gcig ni za bar byed/_/shi nas gcan gzan rigs su ltung /_/dri mi zhim pa smad pa dang /_/glen pa dag tu skye bar 'gyur/_/
[\[284a.7\]]phal cher gdol pa'i rigs su skye/_/rngon pa sme sha can rigs dang /_/yang na 'phra men rigs su skye/_/sha za'i rigs su skye bar 'gyur/_/srin po byi la la sogs pa'i/_/mngal kun du ni skye bar 'gyur/_/glang po 'ching dang sprin chen dang /_/
[\[284b\]]
[\[284b.1\]]sor mo'i phreng ba'i mdo dag dang /_/lang kar gshegs pa'i mdo 'di las/_/ngas ni sha rnams rab tu bkag_/sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang /_/nyan thos rnams kyis smad pa dang /_/ngo mi tsha bar za ba ni/_/rtag tu glen par skye bar 'gyur/_/
[\[284b.2\]]sngar yang mthong thos dogs pa yi/_/sha rnams thams cad spang bar bshad/_/rtog ge rnams kyis ma rtogs pas/_/de phyir sha zan gnas su skyes/_/ji ltar 'dod chags nyes pa yis/_/'phags pa'i rnam grol sgrib 'gyur zhing /_/sha 
[\[284b.3\]]chang sgog btsong la sogs pa'ang /_/'phags pa'i lam gyi bar chad byed/_/ma 'ongs dus kyi skye bo dag_/sha za gti mug smra ba rnams/_/sha ni sdig med rung bas na/_/sangs rgyas nged la gnang zhes zer/_/zas la sman sten 'du shes dang /_/
[\[284b.4\]]de bzhin bu sha lta bur yang /_/chos shes skyo ba bskyed nas ni/_/rnal 'byor can gyis bsod snyoms spyad/_/snying rje sems la gang gnas pa/_/yongs su skyo bar nga yis bshad/_/stag dang spyang ki gcan gzan rnams/_/rtag tu lhan cig 
[\[284b.5\]]'grogs shing gnas/_/gal te sha rnams za byed na/_/sems can dag ni skrag 'gyur bas/_/de phyir rnal 'byor pa dag gis/_/snying rje'i sems kyis sha mi bza'/_/sha za snying rje shes rab med/_/rnam par grol la rgyab kyis phyogs/_/'phags pa'i 
[\[284b.6\]]rgyal mtshan dag dang 'gal/_/de yi phyir na bza' mi rigs/_/bram ze'i rigs su skye bar 'gyur/_/gzhan yang rnal 'byor pa yi gnas/_/shes rab phyug po'i khyim du skye/_/'di ni sha spangs dbang gis so/_/'phags pa lang kar gshegs pa rin po che'i mdo las sangs rgyas 
[\[284b.7\]]thams cad kyi gsung gi snying po'i le'u zhes bya ba rdzogs so//_//dpal lha btsan po'i bka' lung gis rgya'i slob dpon wen hwi yis mdzad pa'i 'grel pa dang sbyar nas/_lo ts+tsha ba dge slong 'gos chos grub kyis bsgyur cing zhus//_//
